// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — explicit saved bytes replace only source selection, never activation or store semantics.
use super::{Options, Project};
use std::path::Path;

/// Select one complete saved backend, or the ordinary multi-file project.
/// The vector is retained for both compilation and staging: one read, no swap.
pub(super) fn sources(project: &Project, options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Vec<(String, String)>, String> {
    match options.flags.get(flag) {
        None => project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        Some(file) => {
            let path = Path::new(file);
            if !path.try_exists().map_err(|e|format!("cannot inspect saved source {file}: {e}"))? {return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist"));}
            if path.is_dir() {
                return crate::client_compatibility::sources(path).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
            }
            let name = path.file_name().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).ok_or("saved source needs a UTF-8 file name")?;
            if !name.ends_with(".q") { return Err("saved source must be a complete Quarry .q file".into()); }
            let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|e| if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound {format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist")} else {format!("cannot read saved source {file}: {e}")})?;
            if text.trim().is_empty() { return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved Quarry source is empty")); }
            // A saved complete backend retains the schema namespace regardless of its archive file name.
            Ok(vec![("schema.q".into(), text)])
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn compile(sources: &[(String, String)], options: &Options, flag: &str) -> snapback4_lang::Bound {
    let mut bound = snapback4_lang::compile(sources);
    if let Some(file) = options.flags.get(flag) {
        for diagnostic in &mut bound.diagnostics { diagnostic.site.file = if Path::new(file).is_dir() { Path::new(file).join(&diagnostic.site.file).to_string_lossy().into_owned() } else { file.clone() }; }
    }
    bound
}

/// A saved file can be used outside a project. Companion code is selected
/// only from an actual current project, never from arbitrary sibling files.
pub(super) fn project(options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Project, String> {
    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if let Some(project) = Project::locate(&cwd) { return Ok(project); }
    if options.flags.contains_key(flag) { return Ok(Project { root: cwd }); }
    Err("no snapback/*.q here or above; run `snapback4 init` or select a saved source file".into())
}

/// A private companion build uses the selected schema's generated contracts.
/// Packaging still records the original host installation, never this scratch.
pub(super) struct Candidate { pub project: Project, cleanup: std::path::PathBuf }
impl Drop for Candidate { fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.cleanup); } }
impl Candidate {
    pub(super) fn prepare(original: &Project, stage: &Path, bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound) -> Result<Self, String> {
        use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};
        static SERIAL: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-saved-source-{}-{}", std::process::id(), SERIAL.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)));
        std::fs::create_dir(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        #[cfg(unix)] snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let mut candidate = Self { project: Project { root: root.clone() }, cleanup: root };
        candidate.project.root = crate::dev_worker::private_scope(original, &candidate.cleanup)?;
        // The worker snapshotter captures the actual import closure and refuses
        // symlinks. Copy those captured bytes, not an unbounded application tree.
        copy_tree(stage, &candidate.project.root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        // Discover the current dependency graph without installing selected
        // contracts in the working project. A failed typecheck still publishes
        // its graph; the copied bytes are checked with selected contracts below.
        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(original) {
            let _ = original.check_entry(&entry);
            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
        }
        candidate.project.write_generated_from(bound, original).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if !crate::native::declared(bound).is_empty() && original.native_manifest().exists() {
            copy_tree(&original.native_dir(), &candidate.project.native_dir()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            rebase_tree(&candidate.project.native_dir(), &original.native_dir()).map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native snapshot: {e}"))?;
        }
        crate::dev_worker::link_dependencies(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(&candidate.project) { candidate.project.check_entry(&entry)?; }
        Ok(candidate)
    }
}
fn copy_tree(source: &Path, target: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(target)?;
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(source)? {
        let entry = entry?;
        if matches!(entry.file_name().to_str(), Some("target" | "native-target" | ".git")) { continue; }
        let kind = entry.file_type()?;
        if kind.is_dir() { copy_tree(&entry.path(), &target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
        else if kind.is_file() { std::fs::copy(entry.path(), target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
        else { return Err(std::io::Error::other("saved-source companion snapshot permits regular source files only")); }
    }
    Ok(())
}


#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn dir(label: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-saved-{}-{label}", std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&root); std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback/generated")).unwrap(); root
    }
    #[test]
    fn archived_names_preserve_operations_and_diagnostics_name_the_selected_input() {
        let root = dir("archived"); let project = Project { root: root.clone() };
        let file = root.join("v1.q");
        std::fs::write(&file, "query alive():\n  return true\n").unwrap();
        let options = Options { positionals: vec![], flags: [("schema".into(), file.to_str().unwrap().into())].into(), json: false };
        let selected = sources(&project, &options, "schema").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(selected[0].0, "schema.q");
        let bound = compile(&selected, &options, "schema");
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty()); assert_eq!(bound.programs[0].program.name, "alive");
        std::fs::write(&file, "not a Quarry backend").unwrap();
        let bound = compile(&sources(&project, &options, "schema").unwrap(), &options, "schema");
        assert!(!bound.diagnostics.is_empty()); assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().all(|d| d.site.file == file.to_str().unwrap()));
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn missing_baseline_names_original_absolute_location() {
        let root = dir("missing-baseline"); let project = Project { root: root.clone() };
        std::fs::write(root.join("snapback/client-compatibility.json"), r#"{"baseline":"versions/v1","since_release":2}"#).unwrap();
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "query alive():\n  return true\n".into())]);
        let error = project.write_generated(&bound).unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(error.contains(root.join("versions/v1").to_str().unwrap()) && error.contains("does not exist (resolved from") && error.contains("snapback/client-compatibility.json"), "{error}");
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
    }
     1	//! Concrete, isolated challenges; finite campaigns never establish proof.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#4-counterexamples-and-generated-tests — only replayed admission is a refutation.
     3	use super::*;
     4	use snapback4_core::{Engine,Store,Context,Value,RuntimeError};
     5	use snapback4_core::schema::{ColumnType as C,Constraint};
     6	use snapback4_core::engine::rules::{self,Probe};
     7	use snapback4_core::ir::{self,Expr,Stmt,Program,ProgramKind,Read,KeyProvenance};
     8	pub const NOW:i64=1_800_000_000_000;
     9	const VIEWER:&str="dev:security-a";
    10	const OTHER:&str="dev:security-b";
    11	// Same verified 1x1 PNG header used by the existing packaged asset witnesses.
    12	const PNG:[u8;24]=[137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10,0,0,0,13,73,72,68,82,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1];
    13	mod paired;
    14	mod timed;
    15	mod histories;
    16	mod assets;
    17	mod column_reads;
    18	mod stability;
    19	mod generated;
    20	pub use generated::fixture as generated_fixture;
    21	#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize,PartialEq,Eq)]
    22	#[serde(rename_all="kebab-case")]
    23	pub enum Reachability { AuthoredFixtureTrace, GeneratedFixtureTrace, TrustedSeedOnly, SyntheticOperation }
    24	impl Reachability {
    25	    pub fn is_reachable(&self)->bool {matches!(self,Self::AuthoredFixtureTrace|Self::GeneratedFixtureTrace)}
    26	}
    27	#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
    28	pub struct Counterexample {pub reachability:Reachability,pub invariant_site:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub viewer:String,pub operation:String,pub effect:String,pub row:Value,pub old:Option<Value>,pub supporting_rows:BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,pub rule_path:String,pub scope:String, pub observations:Option<paired::Observations>,pub history:Vec<histories::Step>}
    29	#[derive(Clone,Debug,Default,Serialize)]
    30	pub struct Campaign {#[serde(skip)] pub history_limit:usize,#[serde(skip)] pub generated_fixture:bool,#[serde(skip)] pub work_remaining:Option<usize>,#[serde(skip)] pub deadline:Option<std::time::Instant>,pub paired_observer_calls:usize,pub history_observer_calls:usize,pub column_observer_calls:usize,pub operations:BTreeMap<String,usize>,pub sequences:usize,pub attempted:usize,pub admitted:usize,pub refused:usize,pub violations:Vec<Counterexample>,pub boundary_failures:Vec<String>,pub unsupported:BTreeSet<String>}
    31	impl Campaign {
    32	    pub fn bounded(milliseconds:u64)->Self {Self{work_remaining:Some(512),deadline:Some(std::time::Instant::now()+std::time::Duration::from_millis(milliseconds)),..Self::default()}}
    33	    pub(super) fn reserve(&mut self,cost:usize)->bool {
    34	        if self.exhausted(){return false;}
    35	        if let Some(remaining)=&mut self.work_remaining {
    36	            if *remaining<cost{*remaining=0;self.unsupported.insert("check concrete exploration work budget exhausted; remaining paths unproved; run snapback4 test for the full campaign".into());return false;}
    37	            *remaining-=cost;
    38	        }true
    39	    }
    40	    pub(super) fn exhausted(&mut self)->bool {
    41	        if self.work_remaining==Some(0){self.unsupported.insert("check concrete exploration work budget exhausted; remaining paths unproved; run snapback4 test for the full campaign".into());return true;}
    42	        if self.deadline.is_some_and(|deadline|std::time::Instant::now()>=deadline){self.unsupported.insert("check concrete exploration time budget exhausted; remaining paths unproved; run snapback4 test for the full campaign".into());true}else{false}
    43	    }
    44	}
    45	fn id(table:&str)->Value {Value::Id(format!("security-{table}"))}
    46	fn sample(kind:&C,variant:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match kind{
    47	    C::Optional(inner)=>if variant&4!=0{sample(inner,variant).unwrap_or(Value::Null)}else{Value::Null},C::Id|C::Principal=>Value::Id(if variant&1==0{VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into()),
    48	    C::Ref{table}=>id(table),C::Text{max}=>Value::String("x".repeat((*max).min(20) as usize)),
    49	    C::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(variant%variants.len())?.clone()),
    50	    C::Int|C::Decimal{..}|C::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),C::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),
    51	    C::Bool=>Value::Bool((variant>>2)&1==0),C::Time=>Value::Int(NOW-1000),C::Json{..}=>Value::Null,C::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),
    52	    C::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),C::ListRange{of,min,..}=>Value::Array((0..*min).map(|_|sample(of,variant)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),
    53	    C::Image{..}=>Value::String("$security:image".into()),C::File{..}=>Value::String("$security:file".into()),C::Video{..}=>return None,
    54	})}
    55	fn needed(schema:&Schema,name:&str,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){
    56	    if !out.insert(name.into()){return;}let Some(t)=schema.table(name)else{return};
    57	    for m in schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.target==name){needed(schema,&m.source,out);}
    58	    for k in t.columns.values(){if let C::Ref{table}=k.required(){needed(schema,table,out);}}
    59	    for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{for dep in kernel::dependencies(r){needed(schema,&dep,out);}}
    60	}
    61	fn snapshot(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    62	    snapshot_mode(bound,inv,variant,false)
    63	}
    64	fn snapshot_mode(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize,timed:bool)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    65	    let mut names=BTreeSet::new();needed(&bound.schema,&inv.table,&mut names);
    66	    if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy{for dep in kernel::dependencies(policy){needed(&bound.schema,&dep,&mut names);}}
    67	    if let Some(mut syntax)=inv.syntax.clone(){super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut syntax,&mut |e| {
    68	        if let ast::Expr::Read(r)|ast::Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=e {needed(&bound.schema,&r.table,&mut names);}
    69	    });}
    70	    if let Some(op)=&inv.operation && let Some(p)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==op){for kind in p.program.args.values(){if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind{needed(&bound.schema,table,&mut names);}}}
    71	    let mut out=BTreeMap::new();
    72	    let mut enum_case=variant;
    73	    for name in names {
    74	        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&name];
    75	        if bound.schema.maintain_for(&name).is_some(){continue;}
    76	        let mut fields=BTreeMap::new();fields.insert("id".into(),id(&name));
    77	        let mut principal_index=0usize;
    78	        for (n,k) in &t.columns{
    79	            if n=="id"||t.engine_field(n)||t.maintained.contains_key(n){continue;}
    80	            let sample_variant=if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){let n=enum_case % variants.len();enum_case/=variants.len();n}else if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal){let n=((variant>>((if name==inv.table{0}else{3})+principal_index.min(5)))&1)|(variant&4);principal_index+=1;n}else{variant};
    81	            let value=sample(k,sample_variant)
    82	                .ok_or_else(||format!("{name}.{n}: asset ledger/required sample is not available"))?;
    83	            fields.insert(n.clone(),value);
    84	        }
    85	        if timed || t.read_lifetime_sensitive() {for (n,k) in &t.columns {if matches!(k.required(),C::Time)&&(!k.is_optional()||variant&4!=0) {
    86	            fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_ref()==Some(n){2000}else if timed{1000}else{-1000}));
    87	        }}}
    88	        for constraint in &t.constraints{if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=constraint
    89	            && matches!(fields.get(field),Some(Value::String(_))) {fields.insert(field.clone(),Value::String("x".repeat((*min).max(1) as usize)));}}
    90	        // Named primary keys used by role probes must select the challenged
    91	        // viewer; changing the enum represents a current-role change while
    92	        // derived assignment/membership rows remain present.
    93	        if name!=inv.table {for (i,(n,k)) in t.columns.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k,C::Principal)).enumerate(){fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Id(if variant & (1 << (i+3)) == 0 {VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into())); let _=k;}}
    94	        out.insert(name,vec![Value::Row(fields.into())]);
    95	    }
    96	    Ok(out)
    97	}
    98	struct Snapshot<'a>{schema:&'a Schema,rows:&'a BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>}
    99	impl Probe for Snapshot<'_>{
   100	    fn exists(&mut self,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[Value],equalities:&[(String,Value)])->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{
   101	        let t=&self.schema.tables[table];let Some(idx)=t.index(index)else{return Ok(false)};
   102	        if idx.components.len()!=key.len(){return Ok(false);}
   103	        if idx.unique&&idx.components.iter().any(|c|c.column().is_some_and(|n|t.columns.get(n).is_some_and(C::is_optional)))&&key.contains(&Value::Null){return Ok(false);}
   104	        Ok(self.rows.get(table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|live(t,r)).any(|r|idx.components.iter().zip(key).all(|(c,v)|c.column().is_some_and(|n|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v)))&&equalities.iter().all(|(n,v)|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v))))
   105	    }
   106	    fn created(&mut self,_:&str,_:&str,_:&[Value])->bool{false}
   107	
   108	}
   109	fn live(t:&snapback4_core::schema::Table,row:&Value)->bool{
   110	    !t.expired(row,NOW)&&t.not_before.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|match row.field(c){Ok(Value::Int(n))=>*n<=NOW,Ok(Value::Null)=>true,_=>false})
   111	}
   112	fn policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value)->Result<bool,String>{
   113	
   114	    let Some(p)=inv.policy.as_ref() else{return inv.syntax.as_ref().and_then(|e|super::concrete::evaluate(e,row,rows,&bound.schema,viewer)).ok_or_else(||"concrete policy expression is unknown/unsupported (including clock and typed literal coercions)".into());};
   115	
   116	    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
   117	    if p.has_reference(){return Err("reference intent requires authorized engine policy replay".into());}
   118	    rules::evaluate(p,viewer,old,&image,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows}).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
   119	}
   120	fn policy_in(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,engine:&mut Engine)->Result<bool,String>{
   121	    let Some(p)=&inv.policy else{return policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row);};
   122	    if !p.has_reference(){return policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row);}
   123	    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
   124	    match engine.fixture_policy(p,viewer,old,&image,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?{
   125	        rules::Verdict::Readable=>Ok(true),rules::Verdict::Denied=>Ok(false),rules::Verdict::Unknown=>Err("policy reference/coverage judgment is unknown; never a refutation".into()),
   126	    }
   127	}
   128	fn program(name:&str,kind:ProgramKind,body:Vec<Stmt>)->Program{Program{name:name.into(),kind,args:BTreeMap::new(),body,read_time_granularity_ms:None,returned_shapes:BTreeMap::new(),native:false}}
   129	fn engine(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probe:Program)->Result<Engine,String>{
   130	    engine_with(bound,rows,vec![probe])
   131	}
   132	fn engine_with(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
   133	    engine_on_store(bound,rows,probes,Store::memory().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)
   134	}
   135	fn hydrate(engine:&mut Engine,kind:&C,value:&Value)->Result<Value,String>{
   136	        match (kind,value) {
   137	            (C::Optional(inner),v) if *v!=Value::Null=>hydrate(engine,inner,v),
   138	            (C::List{of,..}|C::ListRange{of,..},Value::Array(xs))=>xs.iter().map(|v|hydrate(engine,of,v)).collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map(Value::Array),
   139	            (C::Image{..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:image"=>engine.store_asset(VIEWER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|format!("verified image fixture: {e}")),
   140	            (C::File{types,..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:file"=>assets::file(engine,VIEWER,types),
   141	            _=>Ok(value.clone()),
   142	        }
   143	    }
   144	
   145	fn engine_on_store(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>,store:Store)->Result<Engine,String>{
   146	    let mut programs=bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();programs.extend(probes);
   147	    let mut engine=Engine::new_at(store,bound.schema.clone(),programs,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   148	    let mut seeded=rows.iter().filter(|(name,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(name).is_none()).map(|(name,rs)|{
   149	        let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];
   150	        let clean=rs.iter().map(|r|if let Value::Row(fields)=r {Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect())}else{r.clone()}).collect();
   151	        (name.clone(),clean)
   152	    }).collect::<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>>();
   153	    for (table,values) in &mut seeded {for row in values {for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns {
   154	        if let Ok(v)=row.field(column) {
   155	            let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,v)?;
   156	            if let Value::Row(fields)=row {fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}
   157	        }
   158	    }}}
   159	    engine.seed_at(seeded.iter().flat_map(|(t,rs)|rs.iter().map(move|r|(t.clone(),r.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>(),NOW).map_err(|e|format!("generated snapshot rejected by schema: {e}"))?;
   160	    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;
   161	    Ok(engine)
   162	}
   163	fn validate_fixture(bound:&Bound,engine:&mut Engine)->Result<(),String>{
   164	    let actual=readback(engine)?;
   165	    for (name,values) in &actual {for row in values {
   166	        snapback4_core::engine::validate::validate_row(&bound.schema,name,row).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   167	        for c in &bound.schema.tables[name].constraints {if let Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table,index,key}=c {
   168	            let rule=RuleExpr::Exists{table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),key:key.clone(),provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed,equalities:Vec::new()};
   169	            if engine.fixture_policy(&rule,snapback4_core::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL,None,row,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=rules::Verdict::Readable {return Err(format!("{name}: trusted seed did not establish final-exists constraint"));}
   170	        }}
   171	    }}
   172	    Ok(())
   173	}
   174	
   175	fn replay(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,word:&str,row:&Value)->Result<(bool,Value),String>{
   176	    let name="$security.probe";let table=inv.table.clone();let key=id(&table);
   177	    let read=Read{site:"security challenge".into(),table:table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),key:vec![Expr::Literal(key.clone())],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed};
   178	    let mut seeds=rows.clone();
   179	    let probe=match word{
   180	        "read"=>program(name,ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(read))]),
   375	        (ir::ArgType::Optional(inner),v) if *v!=Value::Null=>one(e,inner,v,viewer)?,
   376	        (ir::ArgType::List{of:Some(inner),..}|ir::ArgType::ListRange{of:Some(inner),..},Value::Array(xs))=>Value::Array(xs.iter().map(|v|one(e,inner,v,viewer)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
   377	        (ir::ArgType::Image{..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:image"=>e.store_asset(viewer,&PNG,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.column_value(),
   378	        (ir::ArgType::File{types,..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:file"=>assets::file(e,viewer,types)?,
   379	        _=>v.clone(),
   380	    })}
   381	    values.iter().map(|(n,v)|one(engine,&args[n],v,viewer).map(|v|(n.clone(),v))).collect()
   382	}
   383	
   384	/// Fixture-free campaigns retain empty-start bootstraps and populated role worlds.
   385	/// The two initial states share the existing 512-single/512-pair work envelope.
   386	pub fn generated_campaign(bound:&Bound,campaign:&mut Campaign,matrix:bool){
   387	    campaign.history_limit=64;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
   388	    if !campaign.reserve(1){return;}
   389	    match engine_with(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new()){
   390	        Ok(empty)=>fixture_observers(bound,&empty,campaign),
   391	        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("empty initial campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
   392	    }
   393	    campaign.history_limit=448;campaign.generated_fixture=true;
   394	    match generated_fixture(bound,campaign){
   395	        Ok(fixture)=>if matrix{operations(bound,&fixture,campaign)}else{fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,campaign)},
   396	        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
   397	    }
   398	    campaign.history_limit=0;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
   399	}
   400	/// Exercise every authored query/mutation with cross-principal candidates.
   401	/// This is conformance exploration, not a guessed spec oracle: only declared
   402	/// policies and replayed violations can fail security acceptance.
   403	pub fn fixture_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
   404	    let first=campaign.violations.len();
   405	    histories::initial_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
   406	    column_reads::fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
   407	    if !campaign.generated_fixture {trusted_reads(bound,fixture,campaign);}
   408	    histories::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
   409	    if campaign.generated_fixture {for witness in &mut campaign.violations[first..] {
   410	        if witness.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace {witness.reachability=Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace;witness.scope="actual authored observation from a validated generated initial fixture; fixture initialization is not claimed application-reachable".into();}
   411	    }}
   412	}
   413	pub fn operations(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
   414	    fixture_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
   415	    assets::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
   416	    let fixtures=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   417	    let has_fixture=fixtures.values().any(|r|!r.is_empty());
   418	    if fixtures.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{campaign.unsupported.insert("operation fixture matrix exceeds 1000 rows; provide a focused security fixture".into());return;}
   419	    let mut viewers=histories::viewers(bound);
   420	    for (table,rows) in &fixtures{for row in rows{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!viewers.contains(p){viewers.push(p.clone());}}}}
   421	    let eligible=bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|matches!(p.program.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)&&!p.program.native).count();
   422	    let quota=(8192/eligible.max(1)).clamp(1,512);let mut remaining=8192usize;
   423	    for p in &bound.programs{
   424	        if !matches!(p.program.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.program.native{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: trusted/system/opaque operation",p.program.name));continue;}
   425	        let target=bound.schema.visible_tables().find(|(t,_)|super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t)).or_else(||bound.schema.visible_tables().next()).map(|(t,_)|t.clone());
   426	        let Some(target)=target else{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: no table population for generated arguments",p.program.name));continue;};
   427	        let dummy=Invariant{table:target,target:"read".into(),column:None,predicate:"allow".into(),site:crate::Site::default(),policy:Some(RuleExpr::Allow),operation:Some(p.program.name.clone()),requirements:Vec::new(),syntax:None,unsupported:None};
   428	        let cases=histories::fixture_cases(&p.program,&fixtures,&viewers,campaign);
   429	        let mut exercised=0;
   430	        'cases:for (variant,mut args) in cases.into_iter().enumerate(){
   431	            let rows=if has_fixture{fixtures.clone()}else{match snapshot(bound,&dummy,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));break;}}};
   432	            if !has_fixture{for (arg,kind) in &p.program.args {if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind&&let Some(row)=rows.get(table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())&&let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(arg.clone(),id.clone());}}}
   433	            for viewer in &viewers{
   434	                if exercised==quota||remaining==0{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: operation matrix capped at {quota} calls to share the 8192-call budget across every operation; remaining arguments/viewers not covered",p.program.name));break 'cases;}
   435	                exercised+=1;remaining-=1;
   436	                let mut engine=match if has_fixture {fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.matrix",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))}{Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));continue;}};
   437	                let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: argument ledger unavailable: {why}",p.program.name));continue;}};
   438	                campaign.attempted+=1;*campaign.operations.entry(p.program.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
   439	                if p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query{
   440	                    match engine.query(&p.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(_)=>{},Err(e) if e.code=="E_INPUT"=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated arguments rejected as E_INPUT",p.program.name));},Err(_)=>campaign.refused+=1,}
   441	                }else{
   442	                    match engine.mutate(&p.program.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>campaign.refused+=1,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: matrix engine error {e}",p.program.name));},_=>{},}
   443	                }
   444	            }
   445	        }
   446	    }
   447	}
   448	
   449	fn fork(_bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
   450	    if !probes.is_empty(){return Err("fixture probes must be authored operations".into());}
   451	    fixture.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())
   452	}
   453	
   454	// Authored seed/fixture data is an explicit trusted initial assumption. A raw
   455	// row-read admission witness is failing; column privacy still needs an actual
     1	//! Real operation histories from a complete trusted fixture, including assets.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
     3	use super::*;
     4	const LIMIT:usize=512;
     5	#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
     6	pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}
     7	
     8	pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
     9	    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    10	    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    11	    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
    12	        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
    13	        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
    14	        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    15	    }}
    16	    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    17	    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    18	    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    19	    out.into_iter().collect()
    20	}
    21	pub(super) fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    22	    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    23	    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
    24	        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    25	    }}}
    26	    out
    27	}
    28	pub(super) fn fixture_cases(p:&Program,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    29	    let args=&p.args;
    30	    let mut literals=BTreeMap::<String,Vec<Value>>::new();
    31	    fn collect(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>){
    32	        if let Some(binary)=value.get("Binary") {for (a,b) in [("left","right"),("right","left")] {
    33	            if let (Some(name),Some(literal))=(binary.get(a).and_then(|v|v.get("Variable")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str),binary.get(b).and_then(|v|v.get("Literal"))) {
    34	                if let Ok(value)=serde_json::from_value::<Value>(literal.clone()){out.entry(name.into()).or_default().push(value);}
    35	            }
    36	        }}
    37	        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for v in xs.values(){collect(v,out)},serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for v in xs{collect(v,out)},_=>{}}
    38	    }
    39	    collect(&serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap(),&mut literals);
    40	    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
    41	        Some(match k{
    42	            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
    43	            ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..}=>['z','y'].into_iter().map(|c|Value::String(c.to_string().repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize))).collect(),
    44	            ir::ArgType::Int=>vec![Value::Int(0),Value::Int(1),Value::Int(2)],
    45	            ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max}=>{let mut xs=vec![*min,1i64.clamp(*min,*max),*max];xs.sort();xs.dedup();xs.into_iter().map(Value::Int).collect()},
    46	            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
    47	            ir::ArgType::Time=>vec![Value::Int(NOW-86_400_000),Value::Int(NOW),Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000)],
    48	            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
    49	            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
    50	            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
    51	            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
    52	        })
    53	    }
    54	    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    55	    for (name,k) in args{
    56	        let Some(mut vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
    57	        for literal in literals.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
    58	            let valid=match (k,literal){
    59	                (ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..},Value::String(s))=>s.chars().filter(|c|!c.is_whitespace()).count()>=*min as usize&&s.chars().count()<=*max as usize,
    60	                (ir::ArgType::Int,Value::Int(_))=>true,
    61	                (ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>(*min..=*max).contains(n),_=>false,
    62	            };
    63	            if valid&&!vals.contains(literal){vals.insert(0,literal.clone());}
    64	        }
    65	        let mut next=Vec::new();
    66	        'product:for offset in 0..vals.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(vals.len()){
    67	            if out.exhausted(){return next;}
    68	            let mut case=cases[n%cases.len()].clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[(n+offset)%vals.len()].clone());
    69	            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break 'product;}
    70	        }}
    71	        cases=next;
    72	    }cases
    73	}
    74	pub(super) fn trace_key(steps:&[Step])->Vec<(&str,&str)>{steps.iter().map(|s|(s.operation.as_str(),s.viewer.as_str())).collect()}
    75	fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str)->bool{
    76	    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){return false;}
    77	    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});true
    78	}
    79	fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value)->bool{
    80	    use crate::types::Ty;
    81	    let payload=|data:&Value|matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key)&&column.is_none_or(|c|data.field(c).ok().is_some_and(|v|physical.field(c).ok()==Some(v)));
    82	    match ty{
    83	        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>payload(data),
    84	        Ty::Optional(inner)=>typed_disclosure(inner,data,table,key,column,physical),
    85	        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(payload)),
    86	        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure(inner,r,table,key,column,physical))),
    87	        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure(ty,v,table,key,column,physical))),
    88	        _=>false,
    89	    }
    90	}
    91	fn typed_source(ty:&crate::types::Ty,table:&str)->bool{
    92	    use crate::types::Ty;
    93	    match ty{Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t)|Ty::Page(t,_)=>t==table,Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>typed_source(inner,table),Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.values().any(|ty|typed_source(ty,table)),_=>false}
    94	}
    95	pub(super) fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    96	    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
    97	    loop{let before=out.len();
    98	        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
    99	        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
   100	        if out.len()==before{return out;}
   101	    }
   102	}
   103	fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
   104	    if !out.reserve(calls){return false;}
   105	    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
   106	}
   107	fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
   108	    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
   109	    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
   110	        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
   111	        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
   112	            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
   113	        }else{true}
   114	    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   115	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){
   310	pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   311	    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}
   312	    let initial=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   313	    if initial.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{out.unsupported.insert("history fixture exceeds 1000 rows".into());return;}
   314	    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
   315	    let probes=Vec::new();
   316	    let mut groups=Vec::new();
   317	    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
   318	        let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program,&initial,&actors,out);
   319	        let mut group=relationship_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture,out);
   320	        group.extend(role_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture,out));
   321	        // Diagonal order gives both actors and argument values an early turn.
   322	        let mut seen=group.iter().map(|(_,viewer,args)|serde_json::to_string(&(viewer,args)).unwrap()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   323	        // Give every actor the same first argument tuple before diagonal
   324	        // variation; a referee must not see only the overturned bout by chance.
   325	        if let Some(args)=cases.first(){for actor in &actors{
   326	            if group.len()>=LIMIT||out.exhausted(){break;}
   327	            if seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(actor,args)).unwrap()){group.push((&bp.program,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
   328	        }}
   329	        'diagonal:for shift in 0..actors.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(actors.len()){
   330	            if cases.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){break 'diagonal;}
   331	            if group.len()>=LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history candidate actors/arguments capped at 512",bp.program.name));break 'diagonal;}
   332	            let actor=&actors[(n+shift)%actors.len()];let args=&cases[n%cases.len()];
   333	            if seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(actor,args)).unwrap()){group.push((&bp.program,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
   334	        }}
   335	        if !group.is_empty(){groups.push(group);}
   336	    }
   337	    let mut candidates=Vec::new();let mut candidate_keys=BTreeSet::new();
   338	    // Front-load a small fair frontier of role and relationship changes so
   339	    // ordinary no-op/rejected mutations cannot consume all observer work first.
   340	    for group in &groups {
   341	        let p=group[0].0;
   342	        let priority=ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,kind)|*kind!="insert"&&(!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,t).is_empty()||p.args.values().any(|k|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Principal))));
   343	        if priority{for c in group.iter().take(8){if candidate_keys.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&c.0.name,&c.1,&c.2)).unwrap()){candidates.push(c.clone());if candidates.len()>=LIMIT/4{break;}}}}
   344	        if candidates.len()>=LIMIT/4{break;}
   345	    }
   346	    'candidates:for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(c)=g.get(n){if candidate_keys.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&c.0.name,&c.1,&c.2)).unwrap()){candidates.push(c.clone());}if candidates.len()==LIMIT{break 'candidates;}}}}
   347	    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
   348	    let mut base=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   349	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
   350	        for row in initial.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){for viewer in &actors{
   351	            if !out.reserve(1){return;}
   352	            if policy_in(bound,inv,&initial,viewer,None,row,&mut base)!=Ok(false){continue;}
   353	            let mut witness=Campaign::default();violation(&mut witness,inv,&[],viewer,row,None,&initial,"always");
   354	            if let Some(mut v)=witness.violations.pop(){v.reachability=Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace;v.operation="$fixture.assertion".into();v.rule_path="initial trusted fixture assertion; no authored mutation".into();v.scope="definite violation in authored fixture; initialization trusted, no authored transition claimed".into();out.violations.push(v);}break;
   355	        }}
   356	    }
   357	    observe(bound,&mut base,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
   358	    // Spend half the pair budget on grant changes followed by an operation
   359	    // as the affected principal; interleave first steps and successor names.
   360	    let mut role_groups=Vec::new();
   361	    for (i,(p,_,args)) in candidates.iter().enumerate().take(LIMIT){
   362	        let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
   363	        let rosters=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter_map(|(t,_)|{
   364	            let roles=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&t);
   365	            roles.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))).then_some(t)
   366	        }).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   367	        if rosters.is_empty(){continue;}
   368	        let mut recipients=BTreeSet::new();
   369	        for (name,kind) in &p.args {if let Some(value)=args.get(name){match kind {
   370	            ir::ArgType::Principal=>{if let Value::Id(actor)=value{recipients.insert(actor.clone());}},
   371	            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rosters.contains(table)=>{
   372	                if let Some(row)=initial.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(value)){
   373	                    for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(kind.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(actor))=row.field(column){recipients.insert(actor.clone());}}
   374	                }
   375	            },_=>{}
   376	        }}}
   377	        let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
   378	        let successors=candidates.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(j,(next,actor,_))|(next.name!=p.name&&recipients.contains(actor)&&seen.insert((next.name.clone(),actor.clone()))).then_some((i,j))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   379	        if !successors.is_empty(){role_groups.push(successors);}
   380	    }
   381	    let mut role_pairs=Vec::new();
   382	    'pairs:for n in 0..role_groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for group in &role_groups{if let Some(pair)=group.get(n){role_pairs.push(*pair);if role_pairs.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'pairs;}}}}
   383	    // Breadth first, then targeted and diagonal pairs: no first step owns the cap.
   384	    let history_limit=if out.history_limit==0{LIMIT}else{out.history_limit.min(LIMIT)};
   385	    let singles=candidates.len().min(history_limit);
   386	    let mut setups=Vec::new();let mut role_changes=Vec::new();
   387	    let mut setup_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut role_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut pair_plan=Vec::new();
   388	    for n in 0..singles+history_limit {
   389	        if !out.reserve(1){break;}
   390	        if n==singles&&!candidates.is_empty(){
   391	            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
   392	            'setup:for &second in &role_changes{for &first in &setups{if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'setup;}}}}
   393	            for &(first,second) in &role_pairs{if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT{break;}if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));}}
   394	            let mut k=0;
   395	            while pair_plan.len()<LIMIT{pair_plan.push((k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()));k+=1;}
   396	        }
   397	        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let (first,second)=pair_plan[n-singles];vec![first,second]};
   398	        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   399	        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
   400	        for i in indices{
   401	            if !out.reserve(1){break;}
   402	            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   403	            let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.args,args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history asset arguments: {why}",p.name));break;}};
   404	            let mut prior=if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.target!="read"&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(RuleExpr::has_reference)){if !out.reserve(1){break;}match fork(bound,&engine,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>Some(e),Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);None}}}else{None};
   405	            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
   406	            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){
   407	                Ok(result)=>{
   408	                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
   409	                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
   410	                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   411	                    if sent&&n<singles{
   412	                        let signature=serde_json::to_string(&(&p.name,viewer,&candidates[i].2)).unwrap();
   413	                        let new_media=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|inv|inv.target=="read"&&initial.get(&inv.table).is_none_or(Vec::is_empty)).any(|inv|{
   414	                            let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
   415	                            after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|row|table.columns.iter().any(|(column,kind)|kind.contains_asset()&&row.field(column).is_ok_and(|v|*v!=Value::Null&&!matches!(v,Value::Array(xs) if xs.is_empty()))))
   416	                        });
   417	                        if new_media&&setup_seen.insert(signature.clone()){setups.push(i);}
   418	                        let changed_role=bound.schema.tables.iter().any(|(table,_)|{
   419	                            let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
   420	                            !columns.is_empty()&&before.get(table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|columns.iter().any(|c|old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok())))
   421	                        });
   422	                        if changed_role&&role_seen.insert(signature){role_changes.push(i);}
   423	                    }
   424	                    if sent{changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,&mut prior,&mut engine,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
   425	                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&steps,out);}
   426	                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
   427	            }
   428	        }
   429	    }
   430	    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
   431	}

/// Fixture-free campaigns retain empty-start bootstraps and populated role worlds.
/// The two initial states share the existing 512-single/512-pair work envelope.
pub fn generated_campaign(bound:&Bound,campaign:&mut Campaign,matrix:bool){
    campaign.history_limit=64;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
    if !campaign.reserve(1){return;}
    match engine_with(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new()){
        Ok(empty)=>fixture_observers(bound,&empty,campaign),
        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("empty initial campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
    }
    campaign.history_limit=448;campaign.generated_fixture=true;
    match generated_fixture(bound,campaign){
        Ok(fixture)=>if matrix{operations(bound,&fixture,campaign)}else{fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,campaign)},
        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
    }
    campaign.history_limit=0;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
}
/// Exercise every authored query/mutation with cross-principal candidates.
/// This is conformance exploration, not a guessed spec oracle: only declared
/// policies and replayed violations can fail security acceptance.
pub fn fixture_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    let first=campaign.violations.len();
    histories::initial_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
    column_reads::fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
    if !campaign.generated_fixture {trusted_reads(bound,fixture,campaign);}
    histories::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    if campaign.generated_fixture {for witness in &mut campaign.violations[first..] {
        if witness.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace {witness.reachability=Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace;witness.scope="actual authored observation from a validated generated initial fixture; fixture initialization is not claimed application-reachable".into();}
    }}
}
pub fn operations(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    fixture_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
    assets::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    let fixtures=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    let has_fixture=fixtures.values().any(|r|!r.is_empty());
    if fixtures.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{campaign.unsupported.insert("operation fixture matrix exceeds 1000 rows; provide a focused security fixture".into());return;}
    let mut viewers=histories::viewers(bound);
    for (table,rows) in &fixtures{for row in rows{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!viewers.contains(p){viewers.push(p.clone());}}}}
    let eligible=bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|matches!(p.program.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)&&!p.program.native).count();
    let quota=(8192/eligible.max(1)).clamp(1,512);let mut remaining=8192usize;
    for p in &bound.programs{
        if !matches!(p.program.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.program.native{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: trusted/system/opaque operation",p.program.name));continue;}
        let target=bound.schema.visible_tables().find(|(t,_)|super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t)).or_else(||bound.schema.visible_tables().next()).map(|(t,_)|t.clone());
        let Some(target)=target else{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: no table population for generated arguments",p.program.name));continue;};
        let dummy=Invariant{table:target,target:"read".into(),column:None,predicate:"allow".into(),site:crate::Site::default(),policy:Some(RuleExpr::Allow),operation:Some(p.program.name.clone()),requirements:Vec::new(),syntax:None,unsupported:None};
        let cases=histories::fixture_cases(&p.program,&fixtures,&viewers,campaign);
        let mut exercised=0;
        'cases:for (variant,mut args) in cases.into_iter().enumerate(){
            let rows=if has_fixture{fixtures.clone()}else{match snapshot(bound,&dummy,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));break;}}};
            if !has_fixture{for (arg,kind) in &p.program.args {if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind&&let Some(row)=rows.get(table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())&&let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(arg.clone(),id.clone());}}}
            for viewer in &viewers{
                if exercised==quota||remaining==0{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: operation matrix capped at {quota} calls to share the 8192-call budget across every operation; remaining arguments/viewers not covered",p.program.name));break 'cases;}
                exercised+=1;remaining-=1;
                let mut engine=match if has_fixture {fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.matrix",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))}{Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));continue;}};
                let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: argument ledger unavailable: {why}",p.program.name));continue;}};
                campaign.attempted+=1;*campaign.operations.entry(p.program.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
                if p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query{
                    match engine.query(&p.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(_)=>{},Err(e) if e.code=="E_INPUT"=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated arguments rejected as E_INPUT",p.program.name));},Err(_)=>campaign.refused+=1,}
                }else{
                    match engine.mutate(&p.program.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>campaign.refused+=1,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: matrix engine error {e}",p.program.name));},_=>{},}
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn fork(_bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
    if !probes.is_empty(){return Err("fixture probes must be authored operations".into());}
     1	//! Column worlds execute authored observers, including derived/count/refusal paths.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#121-column-read-intent — excluding a field from a projection must not produce a synthetic whole-row refutation.
     3	use super::*;
     4	fn reserve(out:&mut Campaign,cost:usize)->bool{
     5	    if !out.reserve(cost){return false;}
     6	    if out.column_observer_calls+cost>16384{out.unsupported.insert("column observers capped at 16384 call/world reservations; remaining value worlds not covered".into());return false;}
     7	    out.column_observer_calls+=cost;true
     8	}
     9	fn discharged(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->bool{
    10	    inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true))
    11	        &&!bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)
    12	        &&!bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)))
    13	        &&!bound.programs.iter().any(|p|ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(t,_)|t!=&inv.table)&&super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&inv.table))
    14	}
    15	fn alternative(kind:&C,old:&Value)->Option<Value>{Some(match (kind.required(),old){
    16	    (C::Text{max},Value::String(s)) if s.is_empty()=>if *max>0{Value::String("x".into())}else{return None},
    17	    (C::Text{..},Value::String(s))=>Value::String(s.chars().map(|c|if c=='y'{'x'}else{'y'}).collect()),
    18	    (C::Enum{variants},_)=>Value::String(variants.iter().find(|s|Value::String((*s).clone())!=*old)?.clone()),
    19	    (C::Principal,Value::Id(s))=>Value::Id(if s==VIEWER{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),
    20	    (C::Int|C::Time,Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(n.checked_add(1)?),
    21	    (C::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(if n<max{n+1}else if n>min{n-1}else{return None}),
    22	    (C::Bool,Value::Bool(v))=>Value::Bool(!v),
    23	    _=>return None,
    24	})}
    25	fn sample_rows(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,inv:&Invariant,row:&Value,presence:bool)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>>{
    26	    let column=inv.column.as_ref()?;let kind=bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(column)?;
    27	    let old=match row.field(column){
    28	        Ok(value)=>value.clone(),
    29	        Err(_) if kind.is_optional()&&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].defaults.get(column).is_none_or(|d|matches!(d,snapback4_core::schema::ColumnDefault::Literal(Value::Null)))=>Value::Null,
    30	        Err(_)=>return None,
    31	    };
    32	    let alt=if presence&&kind.is_optional(){if old==Value::Null{sample(kind.required(),0)?}else{Value::Null}}else{alternative(kind,&old)?};
    33	    let mut out=rows.clone();
    34	    let selected=out.get_mut(&inv.table)?.iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())?;
    35	    if let Value::Row(fields)=selected{fields.insert(column.clone(),alt);}else{return None;}
    36	    Some(out)
    37	}
    38	fn same_base_world(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,primary:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,alternate:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,key:&Value)->bool{
    39	    bound.schema.tables.iter().filter(|(table,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()).all(|(table,definition)|{
    40	        let empty=Vec::new();let rows=primary.get(table).unwrap_or(&empty);let other=alternate.get(table).unwrap_or(&empty);
    41	        rows.len()==other.len()&&rows.iter().all(|old|other.iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|{
    42	            let (Value::Row(a),Value::Row(b))=(old,new)else{return false;};
    43	            a.keys().chain(b.keys()).filter(|c|!definition.maintained.contains_key(*c)&&!(table==&inv.table&&inv.column.as_ref()==Some(*c)&&old.field("id").ok()==Some(key))).all(|c|old.field(c).ok()==new.field(c).ok())
    44	        }))
    45	    })
    46	}
    47	// Counterfactual field mutation must not break an authored creator's copies
    48	// or recipient guards. Direct typed payload observations run independently.
    49	fn coupled(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->bool{
    50	    let column=inv.column.as_deref().unwrap();
    51	    let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
    52	    if table.rules.insert.needs_field_value(column,&table.columns[column])||table.update_rule_judged().needs_field_value(column,&table.columns[column]){return true;}
    53	    fn writes(value:&serde_json::Value,table:&str,column:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,program:&str,found:&mut bool,coupled:&mut bool){
    54	        if let Some(object)=value.as_object(){
    55	            for tag in ["Insert","InsertIfAbsent","Upsert","Update","WriteTarget"]{
    56	                let Some(write)=object.get(tag)else{continue;};
    57	                let name=write.get("table").or_else(||write.get("target").and_then(|t|t.get("table"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str);
    58	                if name!=Some(table){continue;}
    59	                let fields=write.get("row").or_else(||write.get("patch")).and_then(|e|e.get("Object")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object);
    60	                if let Some(expression)=fields.and_then(|fields|fields.get(column)){
    61	                    *found=true;
    62	                    if let Some(name)=expression.get("Variable").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)&&args.contains_key(name){
    63	                        let occurrence=serde_json::to_string(&Expr::Variable(name.into())).unwrap();
    64	                        if program.matches(&occurrence).count()!=1{*coupled=true;}
    65	                    }else{*coupled=true;}
    66	                }
    67	            }
    68	            for child in object.values(){writes(child,table,column,args,program,found,coupled);}
    69	        }else if let Some(xs)=value.as_array(){for child in xs{writes(child,table,column,args,program,found,coupled);}}
    70	    }
    71	    let(mut found,mut unsafe_writer)=(false,false);
    72	    for p in &bound.programs{let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();let text=serde_json::to_string(&p.program).unwrap();writes(&json,&inv.table,column,&p.program.args,&text,&mut found,&mut unsafe_writer);}
    73	    found&&unsafe_writer
    74	}
    75	fn run_pair_variant(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,presence:bool,out:&mut Campaign){
    76	    if discharged(bound,inv){return;}
    77	    if coupled(bound,inv){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: alternate value may break authored creator/guard coupling; only actual typed payloads and committed histories count",inv.target_label()));return;}
    78	    if !reserve(out,1){return;}
    79	    let column=inv.column.as_deref().unwrap();
    80	    let kind=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns[column];
    81	    let mut other=match sample_rows(bound,rows,inv,row,presence){
    82	        Some(other)=>other,

    89	    };
    90	    // Both worlds receive the same verified ledger, including the alternate
    91	    // upload. Only the protected row value changes; no fabricated media ids.
    92	    let ledger;

    94	        let Some(base)=fixture else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: media column pair requires the fixture ledger",inv.table,column));return;};

    96	        let target=other.get_mut(&inv.table).unwrap().iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).unwrap();
    97	        if let Value::Row(fields)=target {
    98	            let value=match hydrate(&mut prepared,kind,&fields[column]){Ok(v)=>v,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: alternate media upload: {why}",inv.table,column));return;}};
    99	            fields.insert(column.into(),value);
   100	        }

   102	    }else{fixture};
   103	    let altrow=other[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).unwrap();

   107	    'actors: for viewer in &actors{

   109	
   110	        {
   111	            if !reserve(out,2){return;}
   112	            let judge=if let Some(fixture)=fixture{fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,rows,program("$column.intent",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))};

   126	            if p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation {

   146	                            if let Err(why)=e.seed_at([(inv.table.clone(),clean.clone())],NOW){

   150	                            }
   151	                        }e
   152	                    }else{engine(bound,seeds,program("$column.pair",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))?};
   153	                    validate_fixture(bound,&mut e)?;

   155	                    let current=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).ok_or("column target is absent after validation")?;
   156	                    if policy_in(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,current,&mut e)!=Ok(false){return Err("validated column-world policy is not false".into());}
   157	                    let actual_args=materialize_arguments(&mut e,&p.args,&args,viewer)?;
   158	                    let observed=paired::observe(&mut e,p,viewer,actual_args.clone())?;
   159	                    Ok((observed,actual,actual_args))
   160	                };
   161	                let (a,primary_rows,primary_args,b,alternate_rows,alternate_args)=match (execute(false),execute(true)){
   162	                    (Ok((a,primary,aa)),Ok((b,alternate,ba)))=>(a,primary,aa,b,alternate,ba),
   163	                    (Err(why),_)|(_,Err(why))=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{} / {}: declined column world: {why}",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),p.name));continue;}
   164	                };
   165	                out.attempted+=2;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=2;
   166	                if primary_args!=alternate_args{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired column arguments require a coupled asset ledger; differing materialized inputs declined",p.name));continue;}
   167	                let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
   168	                if !same_base_world(bound,inv,&primary_rows,&alternate_rows,key){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: committed worlds change non-protected base fields/captures or populations; reduction unavailable",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()));continue;}
   169	                if a==b{continue;}
   170	                if !matches!((execute(false),execute(true)),(Ok((aa,pa,aa_args)),Ok((bb,pb,bb_args))) if aa==a&&bb==b&&pa==primary_rows&&pb==alternate_rows&&aa_args==primary_args&&bb_args==alternate_args){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: repeated column inputs/worlds/output are unstable or unavailable",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;}
   171	                let actual_row=primary_rows[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(key)).unwrap();
   172	                let actual_alternate=alternate_rows[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(key)).unwrap();
   173	                out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:if fixture.is_some(){Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace}else{Reachability::SyntheticOperation},invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:primary_args,viewer:viewer.clone(),operation:p.name.clone(),effect:"column read observation".into(),row:actual_row.clone(),old:Some(actual_alternate.clone()),supporting_rows:trace.map(|(initial,_)|initial.clone()).unwrap_or_else(||primary_rows.clone()),history:trace.map(|(_,steps)|steps.to_vec()).unwrap_or_default(),rule_path:format!("authored observer depends on {}.{} while policy is false in both typed worlds",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()),scope:if fixture.is_some(){"primary observation executes on trusted fixture; alternate column is a dependency test, not an authored alternate-history claim"}else{"schema-valid synthetic worlds; authored observer, no application reachability claim"}.into(),observations:Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:alternate_rows})});
   174	                continue 'actors;
   175	            }
   176	        }
   177	    }
   178	}
   179	fn run_pair(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,out:&mut Campaign){
   180	    run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,false,out);
   181	    if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(c)).is_some_and(C::is_optional){run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,true,out);}
   182	}
   183	pub(super) fn generated(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
   184	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))){
   185	        if inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(super::super::temporal::has_clock){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column clock pairs use the campaign clock only; other temporal ticks not covered",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()));}
   186	        for variant in 0..16{
   187	            if !reserve(out,1){return;}
   188	            let rows=match snapshot(bound,inv,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column snapshot: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
   189	            let e=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$column.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column seed: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
   190	            let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)else{continue;};
   191	            for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,None,None,out);}
   192	            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){break;}
   193	        }
   194	    }
   195	}
   196	pub(super) fn fixture(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   197	    let Ok(rows)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
   198	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")){
   199	        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),None,out);}
   200	    }
   201	}
   202	
   203	pub(super) fn after_history(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,steps:&[histories::Step],out:&mut Campaign){
   204	    let rows=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);return;}};
   205	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")){
   206	
   207	        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),Some((initial,steps)),out);}
   208	    }
   209	}
