fn readback(engine:&Engine)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{engine.schema().tables.keys().map(|t|engine.store().rows(t).map(|rs|(t.clone(),rs)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())).collect()}

fn operation_preflight(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<(),String>{
    let Some(name)=&inv.operation else{return Ok(());};
    let p=&bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==name).ok_or("operation did not bind")?.program;
    if !super::census::body(&p.body){return Err(format!("{name}: opaque/loop/scan path requires write-boundary tracing"));}
    let rule=inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("operation policy outside rule fragment")?;
    if rule.any_term(&|t|!matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))){return Err("operation policy requires write-boundary row-image tracing".into());}
    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
    if writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).count()>1{return Err(format!("{name}: multiple target writes need executed-effect tracing; net images do not identify delete/reinsert"));}
    let unstable=kernel::dependencies(rule).iter().any(|dep|{
        let hits=writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==dep).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        !hits.is_empty()&&!(dep==&inv.table&&hits.len()==1&&hits[0].1=="insert")
    });
    if unstable||!bound.schema.maintains.is_empty()||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.sequences.is_empty()||!t.maintained.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())){
        return Err("operation policy dependency may change before/after its write boundary; snapshot correspondence unproved".into());
    }Ok(())
}

/// Schema-only engine challenges. Successful synthetic operations refute the
/// declared table-authority contract, not application-operation reachability.
pub fn run(bound:&Bound)->Campaign{run_selected(bound,None)}
/// `check` searches unproved direct obligations; unsearched blockers remain
/// explicitly unproved. Default `test` passes None and runs the whole set.
pub fn run_selected(bound:&Bound,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>)->Campaign{run_selected_after(bound,sites,Campaign::default())}
/// Continue after trusted observations, preserving one set of work budgets.
pub fn run_selected_after(bound:&Bound,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>,mut out:Campaign)->Campaign{
    // A direct component theorem needs no repeated concrete search for that
    // same component. Indirect observers, temporal reads and actual authored
    // operations below still run; the whole declaration remains unproved.
    let discharged=super::analyze(bound).invariants.into_iter()
        .filter(|v|v.direct_rule==Status::Proved&&v.invariant.operation.is_none()
            && (matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"|"insert"|"delete")
                || matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"update"|"write")&&bound.schema.tables[&v.invariant.table].written_by.is_empty()))
        .map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let mut assertions=bound.invariants.clone();
    for inv in &bound.invariants {
        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue;}
//! Positive campaign evidence only; never a cached security proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#137-wave-19-correction-admissible-starts-and-persistent-refutations — exact source/fixture/executable binding.
use super::*;
use sha2::{Digest,Sha256};
#[derive(serde::Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
pub(super) struct Record{pub fingerprint:String,pub complete:bool,pub attempted:usize,pub sequences:usize,pub coverage_gaps:usize,pub violations:Vec<security::challenges::Counterexample>}
fn selected_fixture(project:&Project,options:&Options)->std::path::PathBuf{
    let flag=if options.flags.contains_key("source"){"source"}else{"schema"};
    options.flags.get("fixture").map(std::path::PathBuf::from).unwrap_or_else(||{
        let saved=options.flags.get(flag).map(std::path::Path::new).map(|p|if p.is_dir(){p.join("fixtures")}else if p.file_name().is_some_and(|n|n=="schema.q"){p.parent().unwrap().join("fixtures")}else{p.with_file_name(format!("{}-fixtures",p.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy()))});
        saved.filter(|p|p.is_dir()).unwrap_or_else(||project.root.join("fixtures"))
    })
}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn capture(sources:&[(String,String)],fixture:Option<&std::path::Path>)->Result<String,String>{
    let mut hash=Sha256::new();hash.update(b"SEC4-test-record-v2/full-default/NOW=1800000000000");
    for (name,text) in sources{hash.update((name.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(name);hash.update((text.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(text);}
    let binary=std::env::current_exe().and_then(std::fs::read).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;hash.update(Sha256::digest(binary));
    if let Some(path)=fixture.filter(|p|p.is_dir()){
        let mut files=std::fs::read_dir(path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;files.sort_by_key(|e|e.file_name());
        for f in files{
            // Follow the same regular-file symlinks as the fixture loader.
            if !std::fs::metadata(f.path()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.is_file(){continue;}
            let name=f.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned();let bytes=std::fs::read(f.path()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            hash.update((name.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(name);hash.update((bytes.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(bytes);
        }
    }Ok(format!("{:x}",hash.finalize()))
}
fn fingerprint(project:&Project,options:&Options)->Result<String,String>{
    let flag=if options.flags.contains_key("source"){"source"}else{"schema"};
    capture(&super::super::proof_source::sources(project,options,flag)?,Some(&selected_fixture(project,options)))
}
fn path(project:&Project)->std::path::PathBuf{project.root.join("snapback/generated/security-test.json")}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn remember(project:&Project,options:&Options,campaign:&security::challenges::Campaign,complete:bool,consumed:&str)->Result<(),String>{
    if fingerprint(project,options)?!=consumed{return Err("source or fixtures changed during test; evidence was not saved; rerun snapback4 test".into());}
    let mut record=Record{fingerprint:consumed.into(),complete,attempted:campaign.attempted,sequences:campaign.sequences,coverage_gaps:campaign.unsupported.len(),violations:campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable()).cloned().collect()};
    if let Some(prior)=matching(project,options){
        let mut seen=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
        record.violations=prior.violations.into_iter().chain(record.violations).filter(|v|v.reachability.is_reachable()&&seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&v.invariant_site,&v.viewer,&v.operation,&v.effect,&v.reachability)).unwrap())).collect();
    }
    let path=path(project);std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let temp=path.with_extension(format!("{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
    let result=std::fs::write(&temp,serde_json::to_vec(&record).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?).and_then(|_|std::fs::rename(&temp,&path));
    if result.is_err(){let _=std::fs::remove_file(temp);}result.map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
pub(super) fn matching(project:&Project,options:&Options)->Option<Record>{
    let path=path(project);if std::fs::metadata(&path).ok()?.len()>64*1024*1024{return None;}
    let record:Record=serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(path).ok()?).ok()?;
    (record.fingerprint==fingerprint(project,options).ok()?).then_some(record)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests{
 use super::*;
 #[test]
 fn a_witness_free_later_run_keeps_failures_and_does_not_promote_synthetic_evidence(){
  let path=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-security-record-{}",std::process::id()));
  struct Scratch(std::path::PathBuf);impl Drop for Scratch{fn drop(&mut self){let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}
  let _scratch=Scratch(path.clone());std::fs::create_dir_all(path.join("snapback")).unwrap();
  let source="table docs:\n  owner: principal\n  read <- allow\n  online only\ninvariant docs.read only .owner = viewer\n";
  std::fs::write(path.join("snapback/schema.q"),source).unwrap();let project=Project{root:path};let options=Options{positionals:Vec::new(),flags:Default::default(),json:false};
  let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);
  let mut engine=snapback4_core::Engine::memory(bound.schema.clone(),bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone())).unwrap();
  engine.seed_at([("docs".into(),snapback4_core::Value::Row([("id".into(),snapback4_core::Value::Id("doc".into())),("owner".into(),snapback4_core::Value::Id("dev:owner".into()))].into_iter().collect()))],security::challenges::NOW).unwrap();
  let mut c=security::challenges::Campaign::default();security::challenges::fixture_observers(&bound,&engine,&mut c);assert!(!c.violations.is_empty());
  let mut synthetic=c.violations[0].clone();synthetic.reachability=security::challenges::Reachability::SyntheticOperation;synthetic.operation="synthetic-must-not-persist".into();c.violations.push(synthetic);
  let consumed=fingerprint(&project,&options).unwrap();
  remember(&project,&options,&c,true,&consumed).unwrap();let prior=matching(&project,&options).unwrap();assert!(prior.violations.iter().all(|v|v.reachability.is_reachable()));
  remember(&project,&options,&Default::default(),false,&consumed).unwrap();let next=matching(&project,&options).unwrap();assert!(!next.complete);assert_eq!(next.violations.len(),prior.violations.len());
 }
}
//! The same fixture loader serves query testing and residency at either CLI entry point.
use super::Options;
use crate::project::Project;
use snapback4_core::{Engine, wire};
use snapback4_lang::Bound;

pub(super) fn engine(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    engine_at(project, bound, options, snapback4_core::now_ms())
}

pub(super) fn engine_at(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options, now: i64) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    let Some(fixture) = options.flags.get("fixture") else {
        if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
            let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            return Engine::new_at(store, bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
        }
        return project.open_engine(bound).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open the store: {error}"));
    };
    let label=options.flags.get("fixture-input-label").unwrap_or(fixture);
    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut schema = bound.schema.clone();
    // Residency defers ordinary maintains until after bulk loading and builds
    // them once; column derives stay installed and maintain as rows insert.
    let residency = options.flags.contains_key("residency");
    if residency { schema.maintains.retain(|m| m.private.is_some()); }
    let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut engine = Engine::new_at(store, schema, bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut names = Vec::new();
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(fixture).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {label}: {error}"))? {
        let entry = entry.map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {label}: {error}"))?;
        names.push(entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_string());
    }
    names.sort();
    // One batch for the whole directory: the engine orders parents before
    // children within a batch, so file order (and read_dir order) cannot make
    // a read-through child or a reference miss its parent.
    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    let mut unknown = Vec::new();
    for name in names {
        let Some(table) = name.strip_suffix(".jsonl") else { continue };
        if name == super::test_cases::FILE { continue; }
        let path = std::path::Path::new(fixture).join(&name);
        let shown_path=std::path::Path::new(label).join(&name);
        let Some(definition) = bound.schema.tables.get(table) else {
            if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") {
                eprintln!("test: skipped {} — saved source declares no table {table}; pass --fixture <dir> to select contemporary fixtures explicitly", shown_path.display());
                continue;
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — the starter's own unmodified sample is skipped loudly; a project's file for a missing table still refuses.
            if !options.flags.contains_key("source") && super::init::starter_fixture(&name).is_some_and(|starter| std::fs::read_to_string(&path).is_ok_and(|text| text == starter)) {
                eprintln!("test: WARNING: skipped {} — the todo starter's unmodified sample rows for a `{table}` table your snapback/*.q does not declare; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's other leftovers", shown_path.display());
                continue;
            }
            unknown.push(name);
            continue;
        };
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture file {}: {error}", shown_path.display()))?;
        for (line_number, line) in text.lines().enumerate().filter(|(_, line)| !line.trim().is_empty()) {
            let at = format!("{name}:{}", line_number + 1);
            let mut json: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(line).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}"))?;
            // A residency fixture is often an exported store: engine-maintained
            // columns are dropped and rebuilt from the source rows. `test`
            // refuses them as an insert and `/seed` do (E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN).
            if residency && let Some(fields) = json.as_object_mut() { fields.retain(|field, _| !definition.maintained.contains_key(field)); }
            relative_times(definition, &mut json, now).map_err(|why| format!("{at}: {why}"))?;
            if let Some(why) = wire::bare_principal(definition, &json) { return Err(format!("{at}: {why}")); }
            let row = wire::row_from_json(definition, &json).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}\n  hint: a fixture row is one JSON object per line with the table's fields and an \"id\"; see snapback4 guide testing"))?;
            rows.push((table.to_owned(), row));
        }
    }
    // A misspelled file name would otherwise load nothing and still pass.
    if !unknown.is_empty() {
        let tables: Vec<&str> = bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect();
        // 0.4.17's init wrote the todo starter's rows beside a project's own schema.
        let starter = unknown.iter().any(|name| name == "todos.jsonl")
            && std::fs::read_to_string(std::path::Path::new(fixture).join("todos.jsonl")).is_ok_and(|text| text.contains("\"id\":\"todo-milk\""));
        let starter = if starter { format!("\n  hint: todos.jsonl is the todo starter's sample that `snapback4 init` wrote; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's {} while they are unmodified (or delete them, and the starter's list/add/toggle/remove lines, yourself)", super::test_cases::FILE) } else { String::new() };
        return Err(format!("{label}: {} name{} no table in snapback/*.q; nothing was run{starter}\n  hint: a fixture file is <table>.jsonl for a declared table ({}), plus {}; see snapback4 guide testing",
            unknown.join(", "), if unknown.len() == 1 { "s" } else { "" }, tables.join(", "), super::test_cases::FILE));
    }
    // Fixture rows take `/seed`'s insert path (defaults, sequences, unique with the author's codes, caps, maintained views), not the trusted bulk import.
    let loaded = engine.seed_at(rows, now).map_err(|error| {
        let text = error.to_string();
        let hint = if text.contains("NOT_FOUND") { "\n  hint: every fixture file loads as one batch, parents first; NOT_FOUND means a referenced or read-through parent row is in no fixture file (check the id)" } else { "" };
        format!("{label}: {text}{hint}")
    })?;
    if residency {
        engine.adopt_at(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot build fixture maintains: {error}"))?;
    }
    if !options.json { println!("test: loaded {loaded} rows from {label} in {:.1} s", started.elapsed().as_secs_f64()); }
    Ok(engine)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000#5-fixtures-and-dev-evidence — relative time leaves, seeds and operations share one captured clock.
/// Only typed time leaves interpret `now`: text and JSON retain their literals.
pub(crate) fn relative_times(table: &snapback4_core::schema::Table, row: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    if let Some(fields) = row.as_object_mut() {
        for (name, value) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(name) { time_value(kind, value, now).map_err(|why| format!("{name}: {why}"))?; }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn time_value(kind: &snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType, value: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) if !value.is_null() => time_value(inner, value, now),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, serde_json::Value::Array(items)) => {
            for item in items { time_value(of, item, now)?; }
            Ok(())
        }
        (ColumnType::Time, value) => {
            let Some(text) = value.as_str().filter(|text| text.starts_with("now")) else { return Ok(()) };
            let delta = if text == "now" { 0 } else {
                let offset = text.strip_prefix("now").unwrap();
                if !offset.starts_with(['+', '-']) { return Err(format!("E_FIXTURE_TIME: {text:?}: expected now or now±duration (ms, s, m, h, d, w)")); }
                crate::dev_clock::offset(offset).map_err(|why| format!("E_FIXTURE_TIME: {text:?}: {}", why.message))?
            };
            let resolved = now.checked_add(delta).filter(|time| (0..=snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX).contains(time))
                .ok_or_else(|| format!("E_FIXTURE_TIME: {text:?} is outside Unix milliseconds 0..9007199254740991"))?;
            *value = resolved.into();
            Ok(())
        }
        _ => Ok(()),
    }
}
pub(super) fn argument_times(kinds: &std::collections::BTreeMap<String, snapback4_core::ir::ArgType>, args: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    if let Some(fields) = args.as_object_mut() {
        for (name, value) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = kinds.get(name).and_then(snapback4_core::schema::arg_as_column) {
                time_value(&kind, value, now).map_err(|why| format!("{name}: {why}"))?;
            }
        }
    }
//! Real upload ledgers for foreign-id and non-image placement challenges.
// @ref LLP 3000.038#3-upload — assets retain upload ownership and verified media type.
use super::*;
const PDF:&[u8]=b"%PDF-1.4\n%%EOF\n";
pub(super) fn file(e:&mut Engine,owner:&str,types:&[String])->Result<Value,String>{
    let mime=types.first().map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("application/pdf");
    let (bytes,name)=match mime{"application/pdf"=>(PDF,"proof.pdf"),"text/plain"=>(b"verified text\n".as_slice(),"proof.txt"),"image/png"=>(PNG.as_slice(),"proof.png"),_=>return Err(format!("file ledger needs a verified {mime} fixture"))};
    e.store_file(owner,bytes,Some(mime),name,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
fn contains(value:&Value,id:&Value)->bool {
    if value==id{return true;}
    match value{Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|contains(v,id)),Value::Map(m)=>m.values().any(|v|contains(v,id)),Value::Row(m)=>m.values().any(|v|contains(v,id)),_=>false}
}
pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    if initial.values().any(|xs|!xs.is_empty()){
        out.unsupported.insert("foreign-asset replay over populated fixtures needs full ledger transfer; empty-ledger challenges run separately".into());
    }
    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
        let p=&bp.program;
        for (arg,kind) in &p.args{
            if !matches!(kind,ir::ArgType::Image{..}|ir::ArgType::File{..}){continue;}
            for mode in ["foreign upload","non-image photo"]{
                if mode=="non-image photo"&&!matches!(kind,ir::ArgType::Image{..}){continue;}
                let mut e=match engine(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),program("$security.asset",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(error);continue;}};
                let sample=if mode=="non-image photo"{file(&mut e,VIEWER,&[])}else{match kind{
                    ir::ArgType::Image{..}=>e.store_asset(OTHER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|e.to_string()),
                    ir::ArgType::File{types,..}=>file(&mut e,OTHER,types),_=>unreachable!(),
                }};
                let sample=match sample{Ok(v)=>v,Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(error);continue;}};
                let Some(mut args)=arguments(&p.args,0)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: asset challenge needs remaining argument fixtures",p.name));continue;};
                args.insert(arg.clone(),sample.clone());
                out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
                match e.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(VIEWER,args).at(NOW)){
                    Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>out.refused+=1,
                    Ok(_)=>{if let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)&&rows.values().flatten().any(|row|contains(row,&sample)){
                        out.boundary_failures.push(format!("{} placed {mode} through argument {arg}",p.name));
                    }},
                    Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: asset challenge engine error {error}",p.name));},
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
}
fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            // A pre-existing seed defect is supplemental; a newly violated
            // assertion or changed image is an authored committed observation.
            if old!=Some(row)||old.is_some_and(|r|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(r),r,prior)==Ok(true)){
                violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,"always");
            }
        }
    }
}
fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
    if !super::super::census::body(&p.body){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: loop/opaque write boundary requires executed-effect tracing",p.name));return;}
    for inv in &bound.invariants{
        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always")||inv.operation.as_ref().is_some_and(|op|op!=&p.name){continue;}
        let judged;
        let inv=if inv.target=="always"{inv}else{
            let Some(stable)=stability::necessary(bound,p,inv)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: unknown/implicit policy changes need executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;};
            judged=stable;&judged
        };
        // A net image cannot identify an intermediate write or implicit effect.
        let writes=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if writes.len()!=1 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}.{}: multiple or implicit writes require executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.table,inv.target));continue;}
        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1!="delete"&&before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: commit-end/implicit retirement is not an executed delete",p.name));}
        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1=="delete"{
            for old in before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|old|!bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].expired(old,NOW)&&!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: delete policy dependency changes before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,old,Some(old),initial,"delete");}
            }
        }
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()&&!bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].expired(r,NOW));
            if old==Some(row){continue;}
            let effect=if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"};
            if !super::super::effects(inv).contains(&effect){continue;}
            if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|old.is_some_and(|o|o.field(c).ok()==row.field(c).ok())){continue;}
            if inv.target=="always" {
                if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);}
            }else{
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table) {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: write policy dependency changes within the operation; tracing required",p.name));continue;}
                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,old,row,prior)==Ok(false)||old.is_some_and(|old|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false)){
                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
fn role_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut groups=Vec::new();
    let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
    if !ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(table,_)|!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table).is_empty()){return out;}
    if !campaign.reserve(1){return Vec::new();}
    let Ok(mut judge)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return Vec::new();};
    for (table,_) in ir::write_tables(p){
        let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&table);
        if !columns.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))){continue;}
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
        for row in initial.get(&table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let principal=t.columns.iter().find_map(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal).then(||row.field(c).ok()).flatten());
            let Some(principal)=principal else{continue;};
            let mut targets=Vec::new();
            for case in cases{
                let mut args=case.clone();
                for (name,kind) in &p.args{match kind{
                    ir::ArgType::Principal=>{args.insert(name.clone(),principal.clone());},
                    ir::ArgType::Ref{table:target} if target==&table=>{if let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(name.clone(),id.clone());}},_=>{}
                }}
                if columns.iter().any(|c|args.get(c).is_some_and(|v|row.field(c).ok()==Some(v))){continue;}
                if !targets.contains(&args){targets.push(args);}
            }
            let mut row_cases=Vec::new();
            for actor in actors{
                let owner=columns.iter().filter_map(|c|t.written_by.get(c)).any(|a|row.field(&a.owner).ok()==Some(&Value::Id(actor.clone())));
                if !campaign.reserve(1){return out;}
                let gate=judge.fixture_policy(&t.update_rule_judged(),actor,Some(row),row,NOW).ok();
                if !owner&&gate!=Some(rules::Verdict::Readable){continue;}
                for args in &targets{row_cases.push((p,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
            }
            groups.push(row_cases);
        }
    }
    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(case)=g.get(n){out.push(case.clone());}}}
    out
}

 #[test]
 fn save_version_captures_selected_modules_fixtures_and_the_actual_release_label() {
//! Advice identifies a risk shape; it never supplies a proof assumption.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#123-actionable-authority-advice — current roles, grant writers, required evidence and refusal oracles.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramKind};
const NOW_FOR_DEFAULTS:i64=1_800_000_000_000;
fn rule_parts(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<&RuleExpr>{match rule{RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().flat_map(rule_parts).collect(),RuleExpr::Not(x)=>rule_parts(x),r=>vec![r]}}
fn role_test(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {rule_parts(rule).iter().any(|r|match r{
    RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{
        if super::privilege::membership_probe(bound,r,table){return true;}
        let columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
        super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,t)|columns.contains(c)&&matches!(t,RuleTerm::Literal(_)))
    },_=>false,
})}
fn reader_columns(rule:&RuleExpr)->BTreeSet<String>{rule_parts(rule).into_iter().filter_map(|r|match r{RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(f))=>Some(f.clone()),_=>None}).collect()}
fn note(notes:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>,site:&Site,code:&str,message:String,rewrite:&str){notes.push(Diagnostic::new(site,code,message,rewrite).guide("security"));}
fn mutable_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->bool {
    let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(name)else{return false;};
    let mut roles=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name);
    if roles.is_empty()&&let Some(principal)=super::privilege::global_principal(bound,name){roles.insert(principal);}
    if roles.is_empty(){return false;}
    let mutable=roles.iter().any(|c|!t.constraints.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(c))));
    fn supplied(value:&serde_json::Value,roles:&BTreeSet<String>)->bool{
        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Object").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Literal").and_then(|v|v.get("Map").or_else(||v.get("Row"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
        true // Dynamic patches cannot establish that role fields are untouched.
    }
    fn writes(value:&serde_json::Value,name:&str,roles:&BTreeSet<String>,update:bool,delete:bool)->bool{
        match value{
            serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>{
                for tag in ["Update","Upsert","WriteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
                    let target=if tag=="WriteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
                    if update&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name)&&body.get(if tag=="Upsert"{"row"}else{"patch"}).is_some_and(|v|supplied(v,roles)){return true;}
                }}
                for tag in ["Delete","DeleteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
                    let target=if tag=="DeleteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
                    if delete&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name){return true;}
                }}
                xs.values().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete))
            },serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete)),_=>false,
        }
    }
    bound.programs.iter().any(|p|{
        let system=p.program.kind==snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job;
        let update=mutable&&(system||!matches!(t.rules.update,RuleExpr::Deny)||roles.iter().any(|c|t.written_by.contains_key(c)));
        let delete=system||!matches!(t.rules.delete,RuleExpr::Deny);
        writes(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),name,&roles,update,delete)
    })
}
fn recipient_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str,branch:&RuleExpr,grant:Option<&str>,roster:&str,effect:&str)->bool{
    if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==*name&&(i.target==effect||i.target=="write"&&matches!(effect,"insert"|"update"))&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::dependencies(p).contains(roster)&&role_test(bound,p)&&kernel::implies(branch,p)!=Ok(true))){return true;}
    // This is conditional lifetime advice, not an inferred current-role
    // contract: the retained grant does not itself consult this mutable roster.
    grant.is_some()&&!kernel::dependencies(branch).contains(roster)
}
pub(super) fn notes(bound:&Bound,exposures:&[Exposure])->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#131-intent-and-diagnostics — equivalence is advice, never refusal.
    for inv in &bound.invariants {
        if inv.operation.is_some()||inv.target=="always" {continue;}
        let Some(policy)=&inv.policy else{continue;};
        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];

pub(super) fn initial_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}
    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};

    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
    let Ok(mut engine)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return;};
    observe(bound,&mut engine,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
}

pub fn apply(report:&mut Report,campaign:&Campaign){
    report.campaign_coverage.extend(campaign.unsupported.iter().cloned());
    let rank=|c:&Counterexample|match c.reachability{Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace=>0,Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace=>1,_=>2};
    let committed=|c:&Counterexample|c.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
    for c in &campaign.violations {for v in &mut report.invariants {if v.invariant.site.to_string()!=c.invariant_site{continue;}
        if v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|old|rank(old)<rank(c)||rank(old)==rank(c)&&committed(old)<=committed(c)){continue;}
        v.status=Status::Refuted;v.counterexample=Some(c.clone());
        v.reasons.push("concrete policy-false snapshot admitted by the actual engine; fixture origin is explicit, not an empty-state reachability theorem".into());
    }}
}

### 10.4 Reduction details and review clarifications

The exact live predicate is `(lower IS NULL OR lower <= now) AND
(upper IS NULL OR now < upper)`, using materialized column defaults just as
the runtime does. Tests include equality ticks, nulls and defaults. The clock
symbol cannot be serialized as a runtime RuleTerm: its identity contains a
colon, which Quarry column names cannot contain. Reversal maps `a >= b` to
`b <= a` and `a > b` to `b < a`, without changing strictness. Only the pinned
server lifetime clock enters this theorem; a query's rounded read-time value
and a device's host clock do not establish admission. Raw probes and every
derived lifetime observer remain separate obligations/blockers. Schedules do
not constrain the time between deadline and sweep. Any between-ticks witness
must execute actual engine calls at both clocks from independently validated
seed ingress; snapshot evaluation of `now` alone never refutes.

Both paired worlds use §8.1's actual `Engine::seed_at`, committed read-back,
typed/default/unique/final-existence validation, and exact text minima. The
named read invariant is evaluated for the same viewer and hypothetical
protected row, against each world's committed snapshot and identical clock;
unknown is not false. These are seed-ingress worlds, not a reachability proof.
Engine contexts are authenticated only. HTTP signed-out and offline/device
histories remain separately labeled. Compare typed canonical object values
and engine-defined ordered arrays; do not sort away meaningful order. Decline
generated ids/opaque work, incomplete results and any observation that does
not repeat when recreating each world. Random/store-specific cursors are not
counterexamples. Explicitly record the unproved sequence/receipt/wire boundary
and excluded timing/meter boundary even when payloads agree. Only a change in
the protected row is an experimental input; other seed rows remain identical,
and referentially invalid absence worlds are declined.

Pullback preserves typed Value variants, full probe table/index/key/equality
identity and row images. Only bare identically typed group columns substitute;
derived id/value, reference and old/next fields do not. Kernel node/atom cap
exhaustion is unproved. The direct edge can be proved as a component; any
downstream derivation lacking a transitive substitution blocks the whole
declaration. Do not infer transitive privacy from that component result.

The missing-intent note includes public/allow tables and tables with any
non-deny read/insert/update/delete rule; a wholly denied table needs no note.
It is emitted once in each check result without persistent suppression state,
alongside any distinct broad-read advice. Drafts are ignored generated output,
never source or an inventory leaf; stale content is regenerated, never a check
refusal. Each candidate carries an author-confirmation comment, and the draft
header warns that copied wrong rules cannot catch themselves. Include ordinary
update candidates; explain omitted captured/transition forms. Full table
read/insert/update/delete declarations (and `write` for insert/update) cover
their corresponding effect in the syntactic audit summary; operation/column/
always assertions do not cover a whole table effect. Declared unproved or
refuted effects are still shown in the invariant verdicts.

### 10.5 Generated histories and production HTTP
### 11.1 Avoid duplicate concrete searches

Installed-doc builder measurements exposed minute-long repeated searches of
already-proved direct read/required-column components. Discharge those direct
searches with the existing reviewed component result, without promoting the
whole invariant or suppressing indirect paired/temporal/history/operation
challenges. For paired experiments, compute a fixed-point observer table set
from the protected table through maintained targets, inherited readers and
all table-rule dependencies. Skip authored operations mentioning none of that
set. This prunes finite experiments only; it contributes no proof assumption
and clears no blocker. Unknown/opaque observers retain their existing limits.

Proved ordinary insert/delete components also
need no duplicate direct witness search. Update/both-image claims are not
discharged by a next-image theorem. Before the unchanged bounded enum matrix,
try one row candidate pinned to explicit policy field literals (for example a
cancelled status). This candidate is not a proof fact: typed/default/final
validation, actual engine replay and policy-false judgment are unchanged.
Preserve every previous matrix sample; failed literal seeding only declines
that extra candidate.

For ordinary updates, prove the policy over both old and next images using
`update_rule_judged => policy.old_and_next`. Substitute an OldField with its
next-image Field only for id or an explicitly engine-enforced immutable local
column: every admitted update preserves those values. Never collapse mutable
fields, reference hops or their images. This is the same immutable-image law
used by the engine's once-judged update rule; it is not a new consistency
assumption about database contents. A named transition allowing only the next
owner cannot prove an old-and-next ownership policy. Owned-field bypass and
implicit writers retain their separate blockers and concrete searches. The
reviewed two-image component may discharge duplicate ordinary update/write
searches only where no owned-field bypass exists. Actual authored operation
matrices and unresolved observer obligations still run.

### 11.2 Faithful changed-image witnesses
# Rules

**Snapback 2 optimizes the edit-to-verified loop — the agent's building an app on it, and ours building it.**

Speed is the constraint. Everything below exists to keep the edit-to-verified loop short
and to stop this repository accreting apparatus faster than anyone can remove it. These
rules bind `snapback2/` and the LLPs numbered 1000 and up; Snapback 1 is baselined, not
exempt, and its numbers may only fall.

Each rule is **[check]** (enforced by `caps`) or **[review]** (enforced by a human).

## Budgets — every budget is a trade, not a limit

- **5 blocking checks, 60s total.** A sixth requires deleting one, in the same PR. **[check]**
- **59 documents in the working set** (`llp/current/`). Today's count. A 60th requires
  archiving one — which is just removing the link. Snapback 2 aims to leave this at 15;
  Snapback 1's links are archived at promotion. **[check]**
- **12 documents in the foundation** (`llp/foundation/`). **[check]**
- **This file: 700 words.** If it grows, something becomes a check or stops being a rule. **[check]**
- **1,500 lines per source file, baseline 204.** Generated files exempt — and generated
  files are built, not committed; so are benchmark run records under `benchmarks/runs/`,
  which are evidence, not source. The baseline is Snapback 1's oversize count on the day
  the rule landed; it may not grow, and `snapback2/` contributes zero to it. **[check]**

## Blocking checks

`caps` · `ref-check` · `snapback2/scripts/test.sh` · `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs --check` · `check-inventories`

## Loop shape

- **Every check reports all failures in one run.** Fail-fast is banned. **[check]**
- **Nothing blocks on anything slower than 60 seconds.** Slow verification runs on a
  schedule, attributed to the breaking commit, and files a bounded set of issues. **[review]**
- **Fix loops get 3 rounds.** Then stop: escalate to a human or descope. **[review]**

## Time budgets

Tracked every run of `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs` (appends `snapback2/metrics/loop.jsonl`).
A regression is a P0 with a name on it.

| | budget |
|---|---|
| Incremental rebuild after a one-line edit | 15s |
| Test what you just changed | 30s |
| Blocking gate suite | 60s |
| Full build, warm cache | 5 min |
| **`snapback2 check` on a program (the agent's product latency)** | **1s** |
| **`snapback2 doctor` boot-to-ready** | **0.5s** |

## Scope

- **`NOT-DOING.md` is binding.** Moving something onto the doing-list means writing why
  and taking something off. **[review]**
- **Delete; don't deprecate.** No compat shims or migration paths before promotion. **[review]**
- **Nothing is compiled, transpiled, or generated at runtime that could be built ahead of
  time.** **[review]**
- **A spec needs an implementer and a date, or it isn't written.** **[review]**
- **Only implemented specs bind.** A document with no running code behind it is a
  proposal, and a proposal cannot block a PR or justify a check. **[review]**

## Agents

- **Agents remove apparatus freely and add none.** An agent PR that adds a check, script,
  registry, config file, or design doc needs explicit human approval saying so. **[review]**
- **2026-09-06 session exception:** the user delegated the search implementation
  decisions; one `snapback2-core/build.rs` may generate pinned Unicode tables at
  build time, alongside the existing package build. No new gate, registry,
  runtime generator, or Unicode dependency is authorized by this exception. **[review]**
- **Snapback 2 LLPs are numbered 1000 and up**; `llp-create` must be told. **[review]**
- **Snapback 4 LLPs are numbered 3000 and up** (`snapback4/`, LLP 3000); it adopts these budgets
  and loop shape as its own and keeps its scope in `snapback4/NOT-DOING.md`. **[review]**

## Shared machines

- Warm, named lanes, one per stream of work. Never clone fresh; reuse the worktree.
- **Never `git stash`.** Use a temporary commit.
- Kill only PIDs you recorded at launch. Never by name or pattern.
//! SEC4: intent quality, typed generated populations, and bounded work.
use snapback4_lang::{compile, security::{self,challenges::{self,Campaign,Reachability}}};
fn bound(source:&str)->snapback4_lang::Bound {
    let b=compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);
    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);b
}
const ROLES:&str="use identity password\ntable roles:\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('doctor','volunteer')\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- viewer = 'service:operator'\n  online only\n";
#[test]
fn equivalent_intent_is_advice_only_and_semantic_reordering_is_detected(){
    let source="table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  body: text <=80\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\ninvariant notes.read only viewer = .owner and allow\n";
    let b=bound(source);let r=security::analyze(&b);
    assert!(r.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE"&&n.site.line==7));
    assert!(!r.refuted());
    let b=bound(&source.replace("viewer = .owner and allow","viewer = .owner or viewer = 'service:operator'"));
    assert!(!security::analyze(&b).notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE"));
}
#[test]
fn per_role_population_catches_private_image_and_file_payloads_with_explicit_origin(){
    for field in ["scan: image <=5000", "scan: file <=5000 ('application/pdf')"] {
        let b=bound(&format!("{ROLES}table packages:\n  owner: principal\n  {field}\n  read <- exists roles[viewer]\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\ninvariant packages.scan read only exists roles[viewer, role = 'doctor']\nquery board(c: cursor ?):\n  return packages after c first 100 by .id\n"));
        let mut c=Campaign::default();c.generated_fixture=true;
        let fixture=challenges::generated_fixture(&b,&mut c).unwrap();
        let roles=fixture.store().rows("roles").unwrap();
        for role in ["doctor","volunteer"] {
            assert_eq!(roles.iter().filter(|r|r.field("role").unwrap()==&snapback4_core::Value::String(role.into())).count(),2);
        }
        assert!(fixture.store().rows("packages").unwrap().len()>=2);
        challenges::fixture_observers(&b,&fixture,&mut c);
        let witnesses=c.violations.iter().filter(|v|v.reachability==Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        assert!(!witnesses.is_empty(),"{c:?}");
        assert!(witnesses.iter().any(|v|v.viewer.contains("volunteer")&&v.operation=="board"));
        let mut r=security::analyze(&b);challenges::apply(&mut r,&c);assert!(r.refuted());
        // A later synthetic witness must not replace the generated fixture provenance.
        let supplemental=challenges::run(&b);challenges::apply(&mut r,&supplemental);
        assert_eq!(r.invariants[0].counterexample.as_ref().unwrap().reachability,Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace);
    }
}
#[test]
fn generated_memberships_refer_to_valid_resource_and_distinct_owners(){
    let b=bound("table teams:\n  title: text 1..80\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\ntable members:\n  team: teams\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('admin','member')\n  unique team, principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\n");
    let mut c=Campaign::default();let fixture=challenges::generated_fixture(&b,&mut c).unwrap();
    let teams=fixture.store().rows("teams").unwrap();let members=fixture.store().rows("members").unwrap();
    assert!(teams.len()>=2&&members.len()>=2,"{c:?}");
    assert_ne!(members[0].field("principal"),members[1].field("principal"));
    assert!(members.iter().all(|m|teams.iter().any(|t|t.field("id")==m.field("team"))));
}
#[test]
fn update_only_column_intent_does_not_refute_authorized_creation(){
    let b=bound("table tickets:\n  price: int\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- viewer = 'service:operator'\n  online only\nmutation create():\n  return insert tickets {price: 1}\ninvariant tickets.price update only viewer = 'service:operator'\n");
    assert_eq!(b.invariants[0].column.as_deref(),Some("price"));
    let mut c=Campaign::default();let fixture=challenges::generated_fixture(&b,&mut c).unwrap();
    challenges::fixture_observers(&b,&fixture,&mut c);assert!(c.violations.is_empty(),"{c:?}");
}
#[test]
fn check_exhaustion_is_explicit_and_retains_no_proof_assumptions(){
    let b=bound("table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  body: text <=80\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\ninvariant notes.body read only .owner = viewer\nquery notes():\n  return notes first 100 by .id\n");
    let r=security::analyze(&b);assert!(r.invariants.iter().all(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved));
    let c=challenges::run_selected_after(&b,None,Campaign::bounded(0));
    assert!(c.unsupported.iter().any(|s|s.contains("time budget exhausted")));assert!(c.violations.is_empty());
    let mut r=security::analyze(&b);challenges::apply(&mut r,&c);assert!(!r.refuted());
}
#[test]
fn board_advice_names_the_unscoped_branch_but_not_resource_scoped_roles(){
    let source=format!("{ROLES}table reports:\n  owner: principal\n  rating: enum('good','bad')\n  scan: image <=5000 ?\n  read <- .owner = viewer or (exists roles[viewer, role = 'volunteer'] and .rating = 'bad')\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\n");
    let b=bound(&source);assert!(security::analyze(&b).notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"));
    let b=bound(&source.replace(" and .rating = 'bad'"," and .owner = viewer and .rating = 'bad'"));
    assert!(!security::analyze(&b).notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"));
}
#[test]
fn dominating_argument_guard_suppresses_excluded_role_and_names_actual_risk(){
    let source=format!("{ROLES}table docs:\n  read <- exists roles[viewer, role = 'doctor']\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation register(role: enum('doctor','volunteer')):\n  require role = 'volunteer' else NOT_ALLOWED\n  return insert roles {{principal: viewer,role}}\n").replace("insert <- viewer = 'service:operator'","insert <- .principal = viewer");
    let b=bound(&source);let mut r=security::analyze(&b);security::add_source_notes(&mut r,&[("schema.q".into(),source.clone())],&b);
    assert!(!r.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION"),"{:?}",r.notes);
    let source=source.replace("  require role = 'volunteer' else NOT_ALLOWED\n","");
    let b=bound(&source);let mut r=security::analyze(&b);security::add_source_notes(&mut r,&[("schema.q".into(),source)],&b);
    assert!(r.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION"&&n.message.contains("'doctor'")),"{:?}",r.notes);
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum Stmt {
    Const { name: String, value: Expr },
    Let { name: String, value: Expr },
    Assign { name: String, value: Expr },
    Push { target: String, value: Expr },
    If { condition: Expr, then_body: Vec<Stmt>, else_body: Vec<Stmt> },
    For { binding: String, domain: LoopDomain, body: Vec<Stmt> },
    Require {
        condition: Expr,
        code: String,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        site: Option<String>,
        /// The author's text for the refusal (`require … else CODE 'message'`); the code stays the contract.
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        message: Option<String>,
    },
    /// A false predicate rolls the transaction back and re-reads `current`.
    Check { condition: Expr, current: Read },
    /// A unique clash yields absence, never the conflicting row.
    InsertIfAbsent { table: String, row: Expr, result: String },
    Insert { table: String, row: Expr },
    Update { table: String, key: Expr, patch: Expr },
    Upsert { table: String, key: Expr, row: Expr },
    /// Target lookup and the old image stay inside the engine. Only a read-filtered result is bound.
    WriteTarget { target: Read, patch: Expr, upsert: bool, condition: Option<WriteCondition>, result: Option<String> },
    Delete { table: String, key: Expr },
    /// Alternate-key delete resolves inside the engine before charging hidden expiry work.
    DeleteTarget { target: Read },
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        scale: Option<u8>,
    },
    Sort { keys: Vec<SortKey> },
    MentionTokens,
    SearchPresent,
    /// A query row assertion cannot classify a metered-out point as absent.
    RequireUncapped,
    DeterministicShare,
    Count,
    Sum,
    CountCapped,
    /// `connected[p]`: how many connections the principal holds open on the
    /// server now; `exists connected[p]` is `> 0`. A device answers 1.
    Connected,
}

impl Builtin {
    pub fn arity(&self) -> usize {
        match self {
            Self::Text(f) => f.arity(),
            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
            Self::JobQuery { .. } => 1,
            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
            Self::JobAs => 2,
            Self::ReceiptSettled { .. } | Self::Extremum { .. } | Self::ProjectFields { .. } | Self::OptionalField { .. } | Self::Calendar { .. } | Self::Civil(_) | Self::Group { .. } | Self::First | Self::Last | Self::Len | Self::Lower | Self::Trim | Self::Sha256 | Self::Sort { .. } | Self::MentionTokens | Self::SearchPresent | Self::RequireUncapped | Self::Count | Self::Sum | Self::CountCapped | Self::Connected => 1,
            Self::ExtremumOf { .. } | Self::At | Self::Concat | Self::Join | Self::Min | Self::Max | Self::Divmod { .. } | Self::Divide { .. } => 2,
            Self::Slice | Self::DeterministicShare => 3,
        }
    }
}

//! Read-only external corpus qualification. No schema or credential writes.
use snapback4_lang::{compile,security};
fn main(){
 let args=std::env::args().collect::<Vec<_>>();let manifest:serde_json::Value=serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(&args[1]).unwrap()).unwrap();
 let mut records=Vec::new();
 for lane in manifest.as_array().unwrap(){
  let sources=lane["sources"].as_array().unwrap().iter().map(|f|{let p=f["path"].as_str().unwrap();(std::path::Path::new(p).file_name().unwrap().to_string_lossy().to_string(),std::fs::read_to_string(p).unwrap())}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
  let b=compile(&sources);let mut report=security::analyze(&b);security::add_source_notes(&mut report,&sources,&b);
  let mut campaign=security::challenges::run(&b);
  if b.diagnostics.is_empty(){match snapback4_core::Engine::memory(b.schema.clone(),b.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone())){Ok(engine)=>security::challenges::operations(&b,&engine,&mut campaign),Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(e.to_string());}}}
  records.push(serde_json::json!({"wave":lane["wave"],"lane":lane["lane"],"diagnostics":b.diagnostics,"invariants":report.invariants,"securityNotes":report.notes,"generated":campaign,"coverage":"original schema; absent policy is explicitly not spec-level verification"}));
 }
 std::fs::write(&args[2],serde_json::to_string_pretty(&records).unwrap()+"\n").unwrap();println!("{} schemas analyzed",records.len());
}

 ## 0.4.34
### 12.2 Reproducible campaigns and reachability

Generated seed ingress contains only authored base rows/fields. Never re-seed
maintained targets or engine-recorded fields. Generated lifetime samples are
live at the campaign clock; principal fields vary independently. Rejected
snapshots are recorded as unsupported, not successful attempts. Existing
fixture histories instead fork the complete SQLite store and asset ledger into
owned isolated memory stores; captures, deadlines, maintained values, lifetimes
and actual media identities survive unchanged. No source store is modified.
Temporary asset directories belong to Store and disappear on drop.

Actors include all principal values in fixtures. Required/optional references
range over fixture rows, not only the first. Short histories visit first steps
breadth first before extending them under a reported work cap. Read and row
assertions are checked after each step. Write/operation assertions judge only
actual committed changes, correct insert/update/delete arms and pre-write
policy state; ambiguous multiple writes, implicit effects or unstable policy
need executed-effect tracing and remain unsupported. A finite trace is never
an induction proof for always or arbitrary histories.

Every counterexample has a machine-readable origin: authored trace from trusted
fixture, trusted-seed-only, or synthetic operation. Only the first establishes
application reachability from the recorded trusted starting state and fails
normal check/test. Seed/synthetic witnesses remain refutations of all-snapshot
claims, visible with --all and JSON, without failing acceptance. Unproved
reachability is never asserted unreachable as a mathematical theorem. Check
loads the same fixture base for reachable histories as test, without HTTP or
business-case execution. A stronger witness must replace a synthetic witness
for the same declaration. --all exposes all witnesses and never changes which
ones fail. No unreachable refutation may print pass/ok beside exit 1.

### 12.3 Actionable authority advice

N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION identifies authored caller operations that write role
or group grants trusted by later rules while the effective admission does not
establish independent authority to confer that particular grant. Include
self-signup roles and transitions where a caller can confer a role they lack.
A trusted service seat or an independently authorized administrator may appoint
other roles; holding an unrelated ordinary role does not establish delegation.
Advice names the operation, grant and gate and points to guide staging's
operator first-admin recipe. It is advice, never an implicit runtime refusal
or a proof of malicious intent. Legitimate open enrollment can be intentional.

N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE requires an actually mutable/revocable current-role
population and a retained principal/grant branch lacking that role recheck;
cover read AND write branches. Immutable/static role populations do not fire.
N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE excludes public populations and caller-owned role lookups;
only an absent/hidden distinction crossing a narrower private audience warrants
advice. Every security note has a real source location and contributes to the
reported note count. Rule printing preserves connective precedence. Identity
429 responses include a conservative retry delay covering the rate window;
the existing limits and authentication semantics are unchanged.

### 12.4 Evidence

Qualification uses read-only copies of all ten wave-17 high lanes and their
spec-authored invariant sets, plus all 150 earlier schemas. Report caught,
partial and not caught separately; all-snapshot witnesses alone do not count
as a reachable detection. Preserve bad/fixed controls, note counts and source
hashes. The open qualification-gap ticket remains open for unresolved whole
proof/device/opaque obligations. No zero-hole claim follows from finite tests.


### 12.5 SEC3 reduction details
### 10.1 Separate observer obligations

Report individual observer obligations as well as the whole verdict. A proved
component never clears another unresolved component. Ordinary point, scan,
planned union/projection, count, sum and existence reads consume authorized
rows; pure computation over those rows cannot reveal an unauthorized payload.
The reduction concerns logical data, not the already excluded examined-count,
meter, physical timing or the separately unproved global wire activity path.
Context changes, native programs and external workers are never pure reads.

For a maintained count or sum with ordinary bare group keys, pull the derived
read predicate back to the source row by substituting its group columns.
Prove that predicate implies the source invariant using the existing exhaustive
kernel. Do not assume the source read rule protects an explicitly broader
aggregate. Lost source fields, value-dependent derived rules, aliases,
row-keyed/private/captured maintenance, temporal contributions and downstream
derived observers without a checked substitution stay unproved with the exact
edge and an author repair. A filter can narrow contributors, but is not used
as an assumption in this first reduction. No maintenance consistency axiom is
added to the propositional kernel.

Generate paired in-memory engine worlds, identical except for one protected
row, only when both worlds seed and validate and the privacy policy is false
in both. Execute the same authored operation with identical arguments/viewer
and clock. Different returned data or refusal code/message is a concrete
logical-existence counterexample; ignore sequence numbers, receipts, meters
and timing. Keep both executions and inputs in the witness. An engine/storage
error or unsupported policy evaluation cannot refute. A passing finite paired
campaign proves nothing. Maintenance is rebuilt independently in each world;
never seed a fabricated derived value. Unsupported referential/final-existence
worlds are declined. This tests count/sum/existence, mutation returns and
collision/refusal observations without guessing which successful call is bad.

    11	pub(super) fn report(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,options:&Options)->Report{
    12	    let mut report=security::analyze(bound);
    13	    if let Ok(sources)=project.sources(){security::add_source_notes(&mut report,&sources,bound);}
    14	    let cached=record::matching(project,options);
    15	    let known_failure=cached.as_ref().is_some_and(|r|!r.violations.is_empty())&&!options.flags.contains_key("security-full");
    16	    let mut campaign=if options.flags.contains_key("security-full"){security::challenges::Campaign::default()}else{security::challenges::Campaign::bounded(1500)};
    17	    if known_failure {campaign.work_remaining=Some(0);campaign.unsupported.insert("matching full-test refutations retained; bounded resampling skipped; repair and rerun snapback4 test".into());}
    18	    if !bound.invariants.is_empty()&&!known_failure{
    19	        let mut selection=options.clone();
    20	        if !selection.flags.contains_key("fixture")&&project.root.join("fixtures").is_dir(){selection.flags.insert("fixture".into(),project.root.join("fixtures").to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
    21	        if selection.flags.contains_key("fixture"){
    22	            match super::test_fixture::engine_at(project,bound,&selection,1_800_000_000_000){
    23	                Ok(fixture)=>if bound.schema.tables.keys().any(|t|fixture.store().rows(t).is_ok_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())){security::challenges::fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,&mut campaign)}else{security::challenges::generated_campaign(bound,&mut campaign,false)},
    24	                Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("fixture reachability not covered: {why}"));}
    25	            }
    26	        }else{security::challenges::generated_campaign(bound,&mut campaign,false);}
    27	    }
    28	    let sites=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved&&!campaign.violations.iter().any(|c|c.invariant_site==v.invariant.site.to_string()&&c.reachability.is_reachable())).map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect();
    29	    let campaign=security::challenges::run_selected_after(bound,Some(&sites),campaign);
    30	    security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&campaign);
    31	    if let Some(record)=cached {security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&security::challenges::Campaign{violations:record.violations,..Default::default()});}
    32	    report
    33	}

**NOT READY** at `b7685e4d03`. Two material blockers:

1. **P1 — Copied-owner protection misses ordinary compiled inserts.**  
   [column_reads.rs:59](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/column_reads.rs:59) inspects only inline `Object` write payloads. The compiler stores insert payloads in a temporary and inserts a `Variable` ([program.rs:1150](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program.rs:1150)), so the coupling check misses the copied owner.

   Consequently, the alternate world changes immutable `reports.homeowner` while preserving `homes.owner`, then labels the resulting difference a failing generated-fixture disclosure. The safe self-filtered query is falsely accused.

   The supplied regression **fails**, both in the suite and independently. From `snapback4/`:

   ```sh
   timeout 210s env CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 CARGO_PROFILE_DEV_DEBUG=0 CARGO_PROFILE_TEST_DEBUG=0 CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=8 cargo test -p snapback4-lang --test security_wave19 an_alternate_copied_immutable_owner_cannot_invent_a_disclosure -- --exact
   ```

2. **P1 — Successfully persisted refutations can silently disappear from check.**  
   [trusted_reads:465](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges.rs:465) copies the entire fixture into every row witness. The writer accepts the resulting record, but [record.rs:46](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/security/record.rs:46) silently ignores records exceeding 64 MiB.

   **Confirmed repro:** a `docs` table with `owner: principal`, `body: text <=400`, `read <- allow`, and `invariant docs.read only .owner = viewer`; supply 600 distinct fixture rows owned by `dev:owner`, each with 300 body characters. `test --fixture-only` exits **1** and saves a **137,661,159-byte** record. Without changing source, fixtures, or executable, `check` exits **0**, printing **“0 refuted.”** Its bounded campaign exhausts before rediscovering the authored fixture violations.

Validation: language wave-19 suite **10 passed, 1 failed**; CLI wave-19 suite **6 passed**. No source changes or prior-review/report reads.

These are source-readiness failures, independent of the separately pending cross-platform qualification.
